-1,\n        )\n        assert output_absmax.shape == (input.numel() * blocks_per_emb,)\n\n        output_quant_state = copy.deepcopy(self.weight.quant_state)\n        output_quant_state.absmax = output_absmax\n        output_quant_state.shape = torch.Size((*input.shape, self.embedding_dim))\n\n        output = bnb.functional.dequantize_4bit(output_4bit, output_quant_state)\n        assert output.shape == (*input.shape, self.embedding_dim)\n\n        return output.to(self.dtype)\n\n    def _save_to_state_dict(self, destination, prefix, keep_vars):\n        raise Attention uses ``eager`` (better numerical match to the fp16\nreference than SDPA in practice for this stack).\n\n``get_compressed_size`` sums unique tensor storages (4-bit payloads + quant_state, F\nimport bitsandbytes as bnb\nfrom bitsandbytes.nn.modules import Int8Params, Params4bit\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, BitsAndBytesConfig\n\nMODEL_ID = token embeddings (tied weights).\"\"\"\n\n    __slots__ = (\"emb\", \"_w_deq\")\n\n    def __init__(self, emb8: bnb.nn.Embedding8bit) -> None:\n        super().__init__()\n        self.emb = emb8\n        self._w_deq: torch.Tensor | None = None\n\n    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\n        if self._w_deq is None:\n            rows = self.emb.weight.data\n            scb = if qs is None:\n        return 0\n    n = 0\n    for attr in (\"absmax\", \"code\"):\n        t = getattr(qs, attr, None)\n        if t is not None and isinstance(t, torch.Tensor):\n            n += t.numel() * t.element_size()\n    off = getattr(qs, \"offset\", None)\n    if off is not None and isinstance(off, torch.Tensor):\n        n += off.numel() * off.element_size()\n    s2 = getattr(qs, \"state2\", None)\n    if s2 is not None:\n        n += _quant_state_bytes(s2)\n    return n\n\n\ndef _param_storage_bytes(p: torch.nn.Parameter) -> int:\n    if isinstance(p, Params4bit) and getattr(p, \"bnb_quantized\", False):\n        return p.data.numel() * p.data.element_size() + _quant_state_bytes(p.quant_state)\n    if isinstance(p, Int8Params):\n        b = p.data.numel() * p.data.element_size()\n        scb = getattr(p, \"SCB\", None)\n        if scb is not None:\n            b += scb.numel() * scb.element_size()\n        return b\n    return p.numel() *  for p in model.parameters():\n        ptr = p.data_ptr()\n        if ptr in seen:\n            continue\n        seen.add(ptr)\n        total += _param_storage_bytes(p)\n    for buf in model.buffers():\n        total += buf.numel() * buf.element_size()\n    return total\n\n\ndef _patch_int8_embedding(model: AutoModelForCausalLM) -> None:\n    old = model.model.embed_tokens\n    new = bnb.nn.Embedding8bit(\n        old.num_embeddings,\n        old.embedding_dim,\n        device=\"cpu\",\n        dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n    )\n    new.load_state_dict(old.state_dict())\n    new = new.to(torch.device(\"cuda\"))\n    model.model.embed_tokens = new\n    model.lm_head = LmHeadFromInt8Embed(new).to(torch.device(\"cuda\"))\n    # Weights are no longer tied to a single Parameter
